## Other research files — archives, not access-control partitions

The following research progress is preserved as historical knowledge and can be resumed **only when the PI requests a topic switch**. The older series names/numbers are retained solely for provenance; do not rename, re-number or erase existing work.

- **SHIP**: `findings/ship-memory-STATE.md`; SHIP-I226 last completed, memory-compression candidates and negative fingerprint result archived.
- **vFHE**: `findings/vfhe-cross-crt-state.md`; V43 last completed, author's October 2026 range-PIoP revision resolves the historical CRT counterexample.
- **General historical series**: `findings/RESEARCH_PROGRAM_INTEGRATION_2026-10-15.md`, `findings/OPEN_RESEARCH_BRANCHES.md`; original global I1–I192 retained as history.
- **EXPLORE**: `findings/explore-STATE.md` is a dormant draft, not a required separate role, MCP instance or isolation policy.
- **Old isolation registry/spec**: `findings/RESEARCH_TRACK_INDEX.md`, `findings/TRACK_ISOLATION_POLICY.md`, `findings/MCP_TRACK_GUARD_CODEX_SPEC.md` are **historical/retired** and are not startup requirements.

## Simple workflow

1. Read `RESEARCH_PHILOSOPHY.md`, `AGENT.md`, `START_RESEARCH_SESSION.md`, then THIS `STATE.md` and the relevant topic findings/failures.
2. Work only on the current PI-selected subject. "继续" retains it; an explicit PI request can switch to another existing topic or initiate new research. Save a proper checkpoint and update THIS state when switching.
3. Store important paper evidence, derivations and falsified hypotheses in topical `findings/` and `failures/`; update topic progress AND `STATE.md` as appropriate. No requirement for track-prefix-only write authorization.
4. Follow read-before-write and verification. Current legacy GitHub Notes writer has no caller-supplied atomic expected_sha; do not run concurrent writers. Never claim a server-side write lock.
5. Human PI makes contribution/pivot decisions. Preserve source provenance and the constructive-synthesis lesson; do not automatically continue parked mechanisms.

## Preservation

The previous long root state is archived at `findings/legacy-global-STATE-before-track-isolation-2026-10-15.md`. All prior CGS, SHIP, vFHE, EXPLORE, and other findings/failures and commits remain intact. No local Codex branch/source code was modified by this GitHub note change.

Full finding: findings/cgs-41-gaussian-rejection-acceptance-gate.md. RESULT: a mathematical IDEAL-MPC sampler can draw private proposal X~D_(Z^n,sigma0 Bpub I), privately accept with probability exp[-pi X^T(C^-1−Bpub^-2 I)X/sigma0²] for secret C=Bpub²I−Sigma_s. Source Lemma7.1 p30 has coefficient bound |s_ij|<=Bmlwe and Bpub²>kn²Bmlwe²+1. Poisson summation + product determinant inequality prove acceptance alpha>=sqrt(1−tr(Sigma_s)/Bpub²)/(1+epsilon_prop)>1/[Bpub(1+epsilon_prop)], since tr(Sigma_s)=n sum_i||s_i||²<=kn²Bmlwe². Thus no generic exponential-dimension rejection penalty under source's generous Bpub if Bpub polynomial and proposal theta correction bounded. This is a known rejection technique / narrow acceptance proof, not a new secure protocol.

Toy n8,k2,Bmlwe1,Bpub12,sigma0=4: trace Sigma=24, analytic smooth determinant acceptance 0.91904991 and rigorous trace bound0.91287093, proposal dual-theta correction <10^-3142; 120000 integer proposal trials accepted 110311 (0.91925833). Proposal truncation tail <3.94e-35. This replaces the earlier coarse 1/Bpub acceptance lower bound; it is NOT a new Gaussian primitive nor full security theorem, and it relies on even n. ε_Q is PUBLIC spherical dual-theta correction.

Under an entirely OPTIMISTIC, UNIMPLEMENTED O(n) per-candidate secret FFT quadratic model with two opened field elements per secret multiplication, 64/128-bit field elements would require about 3.5/7 MiB PER SESSION of opened-value traffic; generic dense model 3.5/7 GiB per session. Original O(LDec n) stored share bits at assumed 64/128-bit q are 8/16 GiB per participant, DIFFERENT RESOURCE. All shown 64/128-bit widths are illustrative, not validated source q or MPC field. Proposal generation, private Gaussian rejections, exponent/compare, secure fixed-point, first-success selection, triples, authentication, adaptive all-acting-set simulation and mFHE KeyGen/Eval security remain UNVERIFIED. ## Latest overriding checkpoint: CGS-43 (2026-10-15)

**AUTHORITATIVE NEWEST CHECKPOINT, supersedes old CGS status headings and CGS-42 section above.** Single active PI-selected target is CGS / Okada–Takagi ePrint2025/409, removal of O(LDec) secret-conditioned Gaussian pad preprocessing from standard-(Module-)LWE threshold FHE. **CGS-43 COMPLETED, CGS-44 NOT STARTED.** Original TWO-ROUND no-pad/standard MLWE/any-T problem OPEN. Private interactive MPC is an explicitly relaxed tradeoff, NOT an original-source solution.

**Latest full finding:** findings/cgs-43-private-fft-precision-gate.md. Spectral formula for source negacyclic ring R=Z[X]/(X^n+1), Definition3.1 p12 and Algorithm7 p29: gamma_j=sum_i|hat s_i(omega_j)|², w_j=gamma_j/[Bpub²(Bpub²−gamma_j)], Q(z)=z^T[(Bpub²I−Sigma_s)^−1−Bpub^-2 I]z=(1/n)sum_j w_j |hat z_j|². For REAL secrets/proposals, conjugate frequency pairing yields Q=(2/n)sum_(j<n/2)w_j(x_j²+y_j²). Ideal real-arithmetic secure sharing computes each frequency in three secret multiplications (two squares and one weight multiply) over two nonlinear layers; source per-key secret weights could be pre-shared. n1024 gives 1536 private REAL multiplication gates per candidate, fixed J224 gives 344064 per decryption. Real Fourier/complex roots are NOT natively available in original finite-field Shamir; ordinary NTT gives rational quadratic result modulo p, NOT a signed ordered real Gaussian exponent (n4 exact rational 42/155, mod97 NTT residue 81). Exact n8 negacyclic Gram rational inverse Q=152353/1482633 independently matches FFT double calculation 0.10275840346194912.

**One precise conservative fixed-point error test:** κ128,n1024,k2,Bmlwe2,Bpub2897,sigma0=4,LDec2^20,J224, source COMPLETE hardness/security parameters NOT validated. Spherical proposal coordinate width w=11588. Geometric Gaussian tail union over ALL J*n*LDec coordinates <2^-130 with public |Z_i|<H=72419. Thus ||Z||²<=nH²=5370379838464. If ONLY spectral real weights have abs quantization error<=2^-b (FFT, proposals/squares IDEALLY exact), Parseval yields |Q−Qapprox|<=2^-b ||Z||². Acceptance probability exp(-πQ/sigma0²) Lipschitz π/sigma0², so ALL J LDec Bernoulli decision error ≤(π/sigma0²) J LDec nH² 2^-b. Another allocated error <2^-130 gives MINIMUM sufficient b=198 fractional bits for this conservative bound; b197 fails. This is NOT a universal lower bound, and public FFT root error, secret multiplication/truncation, exponential/compare, share generation and final output still require separate error/cost proof. A native 64/128-bit field register cannot directly encode that real fractional weight precision; multi-limb possible with extra computation.

Let H be public secret-only Gram hint, V=(s_i(rho))_i∈F_q^d a joint module secret CRT component conditioned on H, A uniform independent F_q^(m×d), and E independent iid projected errors with law μ on F_q. chi²(P_(A,H,Y) || P_(A,H)×U(F_q^m))
= CPbar(V|H) [(q CP(μ))^m−1],

with average conditional collision CPbar(V|H)=Σ_h Pr(h)Σ_v Pr(v|h)². Proof split V=V' (error collision CP(μ)^m) vs V!=V' (A difference uniform q^-m), and average h. TV <=0.5 sqrt(chi²). This is an elementary exact pair-collision result, NOT a new cryptographic theorem. If no error μ=delta0, chi²=CPbar(V|H)(q^m−1), and CPbar≥q^-d makes a straight noiseless LHL bound infeasible at m≥d; NOT a no-go for computational noisy decision MLWE or more sophisticated reductions. If μ=uniform F_q, chi²=0 regardless of public hint; intermediate errors require BOTH secret CP and μ's mixing.

**ONE EXACT INDEPENDENT ENUMERATION:** q7 rank d2, V uniform ternary², h=sum V_i² mod7, error uniform ternary; enumerated all A matrices (m1 49, m2 2401) and all V/E, direct rational chi² exactly 4/9 (m1) and 40/27 (m2), identical to formula. Apply previous CGS49 exact n4,q97,d2 full Gram conditional CP=39/6561, projected ternary error supported on 81 distinct residues CP(μ)=1/81, gives chi²=(39/6561)[(97/81)^m−1]. One-factor TV upper bounds m1 0.017133, m2 0.025398, m5 0.046624, m10 0.086762, m20 0.230622, m40 VACUOUS. Without noise bound m2 already vacuous. General Fourier error identity q CP(μ_rho)=Σ_u∏_j |E_{e_j}e^(2π i u rho^j e_j/q)|² independently verified q97. Entire FHE ring/error correlations not captured.

**IMPORTANT GUARD:** CRT MARGINAL uniformity does not imply joint spectrum uniformity. Artificial noise polynomial E(X)=u uniform constant coefficient has E(rho)=u uniform in every CRT factor but its full CRT vector lies on diagonal (u,...,u), joint TV from uniform exactly 1−q^(1−n). This is not source χ, but rigorously prevents one-factor security overclaim. Source original publicly known Gram is a MODIFIED key/assumption and full public/evaluation-key/ODec joint transcript was not proved. 2024 Lin et al. decision entropic MLWE uses particular masked-secret leakage; PKC2026 Boudgoust et al. known-covariance theorem SEARCH high-rank/HNF secret+error; no direct source decision FHE assumption established.

**REPRODUCERS:** /mnt/data/cgs50_noisy_conditional_hash_gate.py SHA256 6f74fd02553d0f46451477d3e9d6e4c69d286932dbbc283b68f85ac7b3300c54; /mnt/data/cgs50_noisy_conditional_hash_results.json SHA256 947ad99c38ca0b9eca907af6b878f504be083b35004e1bde0d7810e1fc606245. **EXACT RECOVERY:** In Rq=Zq[X]/(X^n+1), n even, q odd (including odd composite q), star s*=s(X^-1), h(s)=ss*. Given three hypothetical hints h0=h(s), h1=h(s+1), hX=h(s+X), compute u=h1−h0−1=s+s*, v=hX−h0−1=sX^-1+Xs*, Xv−u=(X²−1)s*. Module rank d: shift one component at a time while others fixed, with 1+2d corresponding hints recover the entire module vector. When the real MLWE error is coefficientwise ternary, recovered s permits sample residual b−As test with real accept1 vs uniform random accept (3/q)^n, advantage1−(3/q)^n IN THIS STRENGTHENED GAME, not source one-hint game.

**PI-selected CURRENT TOPIC:** SHIP evaluation-key memory, original Cheon–Hanrot–Kim–Stehlé ePrint2025/784, separate independent third-party pinned Poulpy `poulpy-fhe/poulpy@250d4392656cd313f92ca66d96d8865702d39ddf`, NOT original authors' HEaaN. **SHIP-I235 COMPLETE; I236 NOT STARTED.** CGS PARK and all I197–I234 remain archived, unchanged. **ONE primary hypothesis:** Try to avoid the I229 key-to-key KSK expense and I232 high-q secret correlated keys by sharing the *same exact encryption mask polynomial* `a` across h32 support-specific HMux GGLWE evaluations for fixed radix group,digit d,gadget row i and fixed input column, while keeping fresh independent encryption noise and original fused HMux online kernel. This could hypothetically allow aliasing prepared mask DFTs, but **FAILS security immediately**. Source in keyset.rs generates `(beta*s,beta)` under output `t_d=sigma_(g_d^-1)(s)` which is IDENTICAL across supports for the same public radix candidate. Second beta gadget input column row i=1 of GGLWE encryption has message `2^52 beta_j`; same a implies public `b_j-b_k=2^52(beta_j-beta_k)+(e_j-e_k)` mod original q2^520, with |e|<=19 => |difference noise|<=38. Reading constant coefficients and rounding by 2^52 reveals beta_j-beta_k EXACTLY whenever β differs; any one differing pair anchors full support-selection recovery per candidate. Secret cancels, not a lattice-estimation hypothesis. This is NOT I226's ciphertext-equality fingerprint, although both warn against randomness/key sharing; the ciphertext bodies and row errors can remain separately sampled. **Source itself fills a FRESH PRG mask for every GGLWE row** (gglwe.rs → fill_glwe_mask_from_source); original SHIP unaffected.

Negacyclic R_(2^156)[X]/(X^16+1), fixed ternary t_d output key, independent e_j with each coefficient in [-19,19], onehot β over 4 candidates at 16 support indices, reused a per candidate across support: **100 trials, 6400/6400 secret selector bits publicly recovered**, no sk or attack solver. Legal bounded-error counterexample suffices to falsify general β hiding, not claim a real q2^520 baseline exploit.

**CONDITIONAL prepared bytes of INSECURE ALIAS-AWARE layout:** original 480 HMux 90MiB each + other2404MiB =**44.53515625GiB**; retain 480 bodies at45MiB, only 15 distinct candidate prepared mask sets at45MiB, plus other2404MiB =>**24.1005859375GiB**, seeming **45.88413% saving**. This only counts theoretical alias-aware prepared key data, cannot use because exact selectors leak; current Poulpy VmpPMat does not implement cross-key mask aliasing. No security-matched Pareto improvement.

**SAFE SEEDED SERIALIZATION prior-art:** Independent pseudorandom a per ciphertext row can be stored as cryptographic PRG seeds (possibly domain separated), *expanded* before gadget evaluation, giving source-shaped raw HMux bytes ~21.09375→10.546875GiB plus seeds, NOT reduced simultaneously prepared VMP array because masks still differ. Noncanonical prepared representations **may still be valid under proper backend CRT bounds and normalization**; this test does NOT prove unconditional incorrect online VMP or source SHIP cryptographic bug. ## Latest SHIP checkpoint — SHIP-I250 (2026-10-16; supersedes I249)

**PI-selected active SHIP ONLY; I250 COMPLETE, I251 NOT STARTED.** Technical failure archive `failures/ship-memory-I250-apeprod-common-term-onehot-selector-incompatibility.md`, commit `2cfd6d39d062825b9854ba2f34bf9032d0fba32e`. Earlier SHIP I249 contribution frontier and all CGS/VFHE/EXPLORE research histories preserved. Parent evaluation-key resident-memory line remains HUMAN_REVIEW/recommend PARK; author h31 security line I245 SOURCE_BLOCKED.

**ONE NEW PRIMARY H250 / one minimal decisive test:** Could apply Bernard–Joye **ASIACRYPT2025** automorphism-parametrized external product (**APEP**) common-gadget-column sharing to SHIP **across different onehot candidate digits** to save >=10% total prepared key memory with no added KSK/secret-selector nonlinear work? Read Bernard–Joye IACR ePrint **2025/163** **Definition4.1–4.2 pp14–15, §4.2 p16** via Research MCP PDF GitHub mirror SHA256 `ce81021fca19132729765ad652a2f4a8f1d27d6b9c7f084777b6386fa5e08387` 37pp, VERSION_UNVERIFIED. In B-J a *fixed* same plaintext `mu` is used with multiple automorphism parameters ψ, so each automorphism-extended GGLWE has gadget columns encrypting `-ψ(s)*mu` and the SAME final `mu`; the last column can be physically shared across ψ, giving `|S|*k+1` vs `|S|*(k+1)` columns for same index. **In SHIP** pinned *independent third-party Poulpy* `keyset.rs` blob `8be6ab92846168330100efeabe9fa19dbd9720e4` generates distinct candidate payloads `(beta_d*s,beta_d)` under `t_d=σ_(g_d^-1)(s)`, with **onehot β_d**. At radix4, candidate plaintext scalar column messages are `(0,0,G,0)` for hidden selection d2; these are NOT one same mu across ψ. Every negacyclic Galois automorphism preserves plaintext's constant coefficient, even for non-scalar mu, so no public automorphisms from one common ciphertext can generate both 0 and G in different candidates (without selector-dependent new ops). With bounded per-row noise E, need `dist_Q(0,G)≤2E`, FALSE for Q2^40,G2^32,E19. **No drop-in B-J common term sharing.**

**Second precise source mismatch:** B-J APEP also removes the automorphism key switch prior to an external product; pinned `poulpy-ckks/src/reference/ship/mux.rs` blob `e9df6b5e4958fea1af6af3334d99fcb8b8f8b5ce` already uses one input DFT hoist, then each candidate VMP, and **public DFT-domain output automorphism with NO online ordinary automorphism KSK**. Thus B-J KSK-saving mechanism has no matching SHIP step. New whole-circuit redesign not excluded but requires costly selector logic and fresh security proof.

**Executed exact witness:** `/mnt/data/ship_i250_automorphism_parametrized_common_term_gate.py` sha256 `d9aca84f97572500f8a51307f84cb0a20a9fd4efb6402c462bed967dfe62135a`, JSON sha256 `05e28d5413036d4f428ebdc900bde75c95f7c2e642194c8ac327554431d7cb9e`. Uniform full polynomial: **800/800** Galois constant-coefficient invariants; scalar plaintext: **800/800** entire invariance; all 4 possible onehot β choices fail common-mu test. Tempting **INVALID/unachievable** bound if sharing were legal: 352 omitted scalar columns ×45MiB prepared =15.46875GiB, total 44.535→29.066GiB (−34.734%); do NOT cite as actual gain or measured RSS. Script is minimal public algebra, not encrypted IFMA/noisy full CKKS benchmark.

**STATUS: FAIL** direct APEP shared-column translation to SHIP candidate beta's / PASS published-paper source and strict algebraic necessary condition. Not a universal no-go of all possible SHIP memory compression; not a flaw in B-J or SHIP. Existing M07/I173 seeded-key on-demand and I205/I208 candidate-selector gate already archived, don't repeat.

**NEXT_ACTION:** Only resume I251 with new credibly **selector-dependent** secure representation that avoids expensive online nonlinear gates and demonstrates >=5–10% fair resident-memory/online Pareto, actual author security proof/source or compiled source IFMA backend. **Tested hypothesis:** With an external irreversible send tool whose target does NOT atomically check authorization, and an authorization/revocation service that changes without giving the tool mediator fresh revocation information, can a controller both (S) forbid every post-revocation send and (L) complete every still-authorized send? **NO.** A's trace is identical between authorized W0 and revoked W1; if send probability is p, W1 safety error p, W0 liveness error 1-p, sum=1; max error >=1/2. This is a classical information/TOCTOU boundary under explicit assumptions, NOT a new cryptographic theorem or proven CoAgent/Cordon bug.

Full CoAgent/Cordon papers inspected, DelAct/DelegateGuard/WTB only abstracts due to access limitations. Thus fully mediated/target-atomic regimes have strong relevant prior art; opaque no-atomic regime is impossible. **No distinctive new feasible A1 algorithm demonstrated**.

**PI switch authorized:** User explicitly said to proceed with **the synthetic-data AI research direction (B1)** following Agent A1 prescout. This is the current task. Preserve all historic EAI-SYS `SCOUT-S54` / `S55_NOT_STARTED`, EAI-P EAI-010, Agent A1, FHE/SHIP/BOOT/CGS/VFHE work and numbering; do not mix tracks or resume parked work. This is one bounded source/derivation iteration, not an automatically launched new model experiment.

**Source-grounded findings:** `findings/eai-synth-b1-source-mechanism-and-moment-boundary-2026-10-25.md`. Negative exact result: `failures/eai-synth-b1-moment-tail-coverage-no-go-2026-10-25.md`. Qiao et al. ICML2026 arXiv2606.13732v2 §§3–4 and Appendix **C.6** already cover biased local verifier causing collapse and multi-silo Wasserstein proxies and **DP Optimal Transport**; Yi et al. ICLR2026 arXiv2510.16657 already cover fallible verifier long-run bias; FedUSD ICML2026 covers principal/residual representation and variance matching. Generic “multi-silo + verifier + barycenter / differential privacy” is NOT novel.

**One H-B1 falsified by cheapest decisive exact toy:** If a verifier knows only aggregate count, `E[X]`, `E[X²]` for `X in {0,1,2,3}`, can it always correctly identify whether real tail x=3 exists? **NO**. Exact `P_A=(0.70,0.20,0.09,0.01)`, `P_B=(0.71,0.17,0.12,0)`. Both normalized, mean `0.41`, second raw moment `0.65`, variance `0.4819`, but rare-mode mass `1%` vs `0`. Difference `(0.01,-0.03,0.03,-0.01)` lies in nullspace of moment matrix for k=0,1,2. A verifier restricted to identical moment transcripts must err in at least one world; randomized worst-case error ≥1/2 on yes/no tail presence. **Classical truncated moment identifiability, NOT a new theorem, no training/GPU.** This does not challenge richer Wasserstein proxies or existing DP count/sketch mechanisms (here `W1=0.04`), and it does not prove actual recursive-model-collapse losses.

**Research implication:** The narrower naive *mean+variance certificate* is PARK/FAIL. Synthetic-data topic remains interesting but only if a precise richer reporting/query model yields an important privacy-communication-statistical frontier not settled by existing DP histograms, secure aggregation or DPOT. In finite-support four-point model there is a single unconstrained degree of freedom after two moments and normalization; a single independent tail-count statistic resolves this toy but is elementary prior art, **not** a publishable contribution.

**STATUS:** `FAIL_B1_MOMENT_ONLY_TAIL_IDENTIFIABILITY / PASS_EXACT_TWO_WORLD_COUNTEREXAMPLE / ORIGINAL_SOURCE_PRIORART_AUDITED / HUMAN_REVIEW`.
**RESULT:** `LOW_ORDER_MOMENT_ONLY_CERTIFICATE_KILLED; FULL_SYNTHETIC_DATA_DIRECTION_NOT_KILLED; NOVELTY_NOT_ESTABLISHED`.
**NEXT_ACTION:** `B2_PRIORART_FIRST_ADAPTIVE_DP_TAIL_COVERAGE_INTERFACE_GATE` **only upon PI continuation**: read original adaptive DP rare subgroup/coverage query prior art and compare directly with Qiao Appendix C.6 and FedUSD mechanics; choose ONE specific permissible query/trust/privacy/communication regime and minimal test; kill if simple DP query solves it or information-theoretic obstruction without a feasible extra interface. **Explicit PI continuation:** B2 Synthetic Data / Adaptive private rare-group verification, following B1. No new GPU experiment, Codex, model download or FHE/SHIP/BOOT/CGS/VFHE/EAI-SYS S55 work. B1 remains `FAIL_MOMENT_ONLY_TAIL_IDENTIFIABILITY`, not global synthetic direction abandonment. B2 is one source-first technical falsification, not a novel published algorithm.

**Sources and provenance:** full result `findings/eai-synth-b2-known-domain-adaptive-tail-privacy-gate-2026-10-25.md`; negative `failures/eai-synth-b2-per-query-privacy-novelty-no-go-2026-10-25.md`. Original arXiv full HTML read: Chen et al. ICML2025 `Scalable Private Partition Selection via Adaptive Weighting` `arxiv.org/html/2502.08878` §§3–6; Asiaee–Aryan UAI2026 `arxiv.org/html/2607.08122` §§4–5; Qiao et al. ICML2026 `arxiv.org/html/2606.13732` Appendix C.6. Kaplan et al. COLT2021 PMLR abstract and Braun et al. ePrint2024/469 original abstract were also checked, without pretending to read their full proofs.

**Primary H-B2 falsified:** Unknown ADAPTIVE sequence of rare-category queries on a fixed, finite, public category domain does **not** need extra differential privacy budget **per subsequent query** if queries use one prior DP release. For replace-one single-atom individuals, h(D) L1 sensitivity **2**. One centralized release `Y_i=h_i+Lap(2/eps)`, independent, gives pure eps-DP; unlimited subsequent adaptively chosen queries whose answers are **only postprocessing of Y** retain the same guarantee. Under epsilon1, d1000, familywise zero-bin false positive target alpha0.05, exact singleton threshold `t=18.420680744`; empirical count c24 gives present-bin false negative <=0.05; c10 detected with probability ≈0.007420658. This is a **standard textbook construction**, not new. Privacy doesn't imply high utility for small c or arbitrary large-support subset queries. Cross-silo aggregation requires a separately specified trusted central curator or secure aggregation primitive; no malicious-party protocol provided.

**Decisive prior art:** COLT2021 Sparse Vector Technique handles adaptive threshold queries; ICML2025 MAD/MAD2R optimizes private unknown/unbounded item discovery, user-level vs our single-record central DP; ePrint2024/469 covers two-server malicious-secure sparse histograms; UAI2026 Causal-AIM already supports adaptive DP workload and reusable synthetic release; Qiao Appendix C.6 includes DP OT. Hence generic “adaptive discovery + silo privacy + long tail” is **not an uncovered primitive**.

**Open issue, NOT proven new:** Repeated generation/training with genuinely evolving private reference distribution, *semantic* rare-mode validation, fixed privacy budget and task-specific convergence certificates may present a different interface, but continual DP synthetic data and existing workload-specific DP inference must be screened before proposing anything. No source-backed nontrivial B3 repair or theorem claimed.

**STATUS:** `PASS_EXACT_KNOWN_DOMAIN_DP_COUNTEREXAMPLE / FAIL_B2_GENERIC_ADAPTIVE_QUERY_PRIVACY_NOVELTY / HUMAN_REVIEW`.
**RESULT:** `ADAPTIVE_TAIL_QUERY_PER_QUERY_DP_NECESSITY_FALSE; RARE_COUNT_UTILITY_LIMIT_QUANTIFIED; SYNTHETIC_B3_NOVELTY_NOT_ESTABLISHED`.

He's literal Algorithm2 tests `N_j+lambda'_t<=T0+lambda_j` BEFORE consuming a record; on test failure it increments segment j with unchanged t (empty segment). The original Dwork et al. mechanism instead consumes x_i before testing close. He Appendix A states unconditional `2T` Laplace variables and, on bad noise event, a trivial `T` segment bound; the printed pre-test does NOT immediately justify them. Distinguish author intent from literal pseudocode. Also printed outer `t<=T` with `t++ ; X_t` permits `X_{T+1}` read at boundary; indexing ambiguity, NOT validated deployed bug.

**ONE B11 source-aligned decisive test:** Check whether the actual code has a guaranteed deterministic K<=T+O(1), or a geometric retry bound, without specifying lifetime of `lambda'_t`. Literal K deterministic bound is FALSE because empty closure has positive probability. Two plausible sampling semantics diverge sharply:
(A) If the comparison noise `lambda'_t` is resampled on EACH condition check, fresh `lambda_j` and fresh `lambda'_t` make first check pass with conditional probability >=1/2 (iid symmetric difference). Every pass consumes >=1 input. With M=T+1 upper bound on record advancements (literal indexing), #segment openings K stochastically <= trials for M successes Ber(1/2): `E K<=2M`, `P(K>4M)<=e^(-M/4)`, `P(K>4M+16ln(1/delta))<=delta`. (B) If each per-timestamp `lambda'_t` is reused on same-t retries while `lambda_j` is redrawn, write `q=e^(-eta/2), a=floor(9lnT/eta)`. For fixed Z=lambda'_t=z>=a+1, initial check pass probability `p_z=q^(z-a)/(1+q)`. The geometric number R of threshold draws has `E[R|Z=z]=1/p_z`. With discrete-Laplace `P(Z=z)=(1-q)/(1+q)*q^z`, the unconditional mean `E R>=sum_{z>=a+1}(1-q)q^a=+infinity` despite a.s. finite completion. This **conditional semantic result** does NOT prove actual author algorithm has infinite expected runtime; lifetime not explicitly fixed.
Thus no unconditional K<=T or blanket geometric-tail certificate follows until semantics confirmed. It is an algorithm-spec/analysis audit candidate, not a novel synthetic-data primitive, DP attack, or established theorem contradiction.

ICML2026 arXiv2606.13732v2 FULL HTML §2/§3 biased selection and tails; Shumailov et al. Nature2024 open full text on finite-resampling Markov collapse without new real inputs. **ONE C01 HYPOTHESIS FALSIFIED:** A constant fresh real fraction α=1/2 and a PERFECT empirical learner guarantee eventual PATHWISE rare-mode support in every generation with probability one. Let true P=Bern(p), `0<p<1`; every gen receives one iid R_t~Bern(p) and one S_t~Bern(q_t) generated by previous model, so q_{t+1}=(R_t+S_t)/2. Finite Markov states `q=0,1/2,1` transition rows `(1-p,p,0);((1-p)/2,1/2,p/2);(0,1-p,p)`. For 0<p<1 irreducible/aperiodic; stationary distribution exactly `((1-p)^2,2p(1-p),p^2)`, so `q=0` recurs infinitely often a.s. and `Pr(q_t=0)→(1-p)^2`. At p=.01, stationary missing-tail fraction .9801. Yet `E q_{t+1}=(p+E q_t)/2` implies `E q_t→p`, and real inflow restores support after loss. This is a standard finite Markov recurrence, NOT persistent absorbing collapse, NOT an author's theorem error, NOT a new contribution.

**Active PI-selected line** `TRACK_ID=EAI`, `ACTIVE_TOPIC=SYNTHETIC_DATA`, `ACTIVE_SUBLINE=EAI-SYNTH`. **LATEST_COMPLETED=SYNTH-C05; NEXT_CANDIDATE=SYNTH-C06_NOT_STARTED / STRATEGIC_PIVOT_RECOMMENDED**. PI explicitly requires any Codex work stay PENDING with no execution or instructions until they ask.

**ONE C05 hypothesis FALSIFIED / exact regime:** Claim: In unknown confusion-matrix private weak verification, detecting whether true rare mode exists MUST invert FULL three-view latent model using higher-order joint moments under pure ε-DP. FALSE for a restricted but meaningful existence test: q=0 vs p<=q<=1/2, two aligned verifier outputs Y1,Y2 conditionally independent given hidden Bernoulli(q) label H, with known VALID orientation lower bound `Delta_i=s_i-f_i>=gamma>0`. Then `C=Cov(Y1,Y2)=q(1-q)Delta1Delta2`, exactly 0 under H0 and >=p gamma²/2 under H1. Release only ONE decision based on noisy first/second sample moments `(mean Y1, mean Y2, mean Y1Y2)` (fixed-N replace-one paired-record L1 sensitivity 3/N), independently perturb 3 components by Laplace scale 3/(N ε), threshold `Ctilde>p gamma²/4`. Mechanism is central **pure ε-DP** by standard vector Laplace + postprocessing. Joint Hoeffding sampling error and Laplace tails prove for BOTH types of testing errors <=β the conservative finite-N condition:
`N>=max(2048 log(12/β)/(p² gamma^4), 192 log(6/β)/(ε p gamma²))`.
Detailed proof: for each of three moments, sampling dev `a=sqrt(log(12/β)/(2N))` (union probability <=β/2), DP noise max `b=3log(6/β)/(Nε)` (union <=β/2). With `h=a+b<=p gamma²/32`, `|Ctilde-C|<=3h+h²<=4h<=p gamma²/8`, safely below the gap between 0 and p gamma²/2. This is only SUFFICIENCY, NOT minimax optimal; strict conditional independence and known gamma are indispensable, and q near 1, correlated labelers and full q estimation not covered. C02 information-theoretic p^-2 necessity remains consistent with the statistical leading term.

**Direct original source prior art/novelty:** Kifer–Rogers AISTATS2017 `A New Class of Private Chi-Square Tests`, original PDF `https://proceedings.mlr.press/v54/rogers17a/rogers17a.pdf` §6.1 private independence tests on categorical contingency tables (original P0/P6 screenshots checked); Kazan–Shi–Groce–Bray ICML2023 `The Test of Tests` original PDF `https://proceedings.mlr.press/v202/kazan23a/kazan23a.pdf` p0 screenshot; Bun–Kamath–Steinke–Wu NeurIPS2019 `Private Hypothesis Selection` `https://proceedings.neurips.cc/paper/2019/hash/9778d5d219c5080b9a6a17bef029331c-Abstract.html` has classic `O(log m/alpha²+log m/(alpha ε))` under its assumptions. COLT2024 Gordon et al. on mixture product identification condition-number and 2024 private distribution estimators are adjacent deep prior art. Our explicit covariance threshold is an elementary application and not asserted as any prior paper's theorem or a new DP primitive. C05 avoids a stronger task (full 3-view recovery) only by narrowing to binary rare PRESENCE; no real synthetic training improvement/cost gain.

**ONE source-first C07 hypothesis FALSE:** Suppose true target/test distribution is a PARTNER-WEIGHTED POOLED MIXTURE `P_pool=sum lambda Qk` of real silo populations, while institutions reveal ONLY the EXACT Wasserstein-2 barycenter `P_bar=argmin_R sum lambda W2²(R,Qk)` and no local first/second moments or transport plan. Is barycenter a sufficient statistic for pooled covariance needed in task-aware covariance selection? **NO**. Cheap two-silo exact witness for d>=2: `Q1=N(−a e1,sigma² I_d)`, `Q2=N(+a e1,sigma²I_d)`, weights .5. Then `P_bar=N(0,sigma²I_d)` from exact translated-Gaussian W2 geodesic, but `Sigma_pool=sigma²I_d+a²e1e1^T` from total covariance. Covariance gap a² in spectral/Frobenius norms. At a=sigma=1, pooled first-direction variance=2 vs barycenter=1. Varying a leaves P_bar IDENTICAL but changes Sigma_pool; pooled target covariance is INFORMATION-THEORETICALLY NOT DETERMINED by barycenter-only transcript even with infinite data. This is a *target definition / between-silo covariance* gap, not DP noise, an optimization failure, or empirical model-quality evidence. Does NOT hold when target is actual barycenter, or parties publish site means/second moments/couplings. Nor should Rezaei ICLR2026 asymptotic risk formula be directly applied to this Gaussian MIXTURE (its original independent feature-entry assumption may not apply).

**Original cited prior art and strongest decisive novelty kill:** Rychener–Esteban-Pérez–Morales–Kuhn, `Wasserstein Distributionally Robust Optimization with Heterogeneous Data Sources`, Management Science2026, original arXiv`2407.13582` https://arxiv.org/pdf/2407.13582 **Example1 'Mixtures versus Wasserstein Barycenters'**, original physical PDF p3 screenshot independently checked: SAME exact statement for equal-covariance Gaussian sources, pooled mixture variance includes between-means dispersion, W2 barycenter variance does NOT; authors emphasize barycenter may be *better* if target is common latent Gaussian instead of pooled mixture. Agueh–Carlier SIAM2011 `Barycenters in Wasserstein space`, original https://www.ceremade.dauphine.fr/~carlier/AC_bary_Aug11_10.pdf §§6.2–6.3 (original physical PDF p18 screenshot) already establishes W2 geodesic/Gaussian barycenter. Qiao et al ICML2026 original https://arxiv.org/html/2606.13732v2 §4.2 Eq19, §4.4 Eq25/Thm5 defines their collaborative barycenter as **proxy** for global data selection; Scheme I instead uses individual silo scores; Appendix C.6 references Lê Tien et al IJCAI2019 DPOT. 'No raw pooling' does NOT mean formal DP by itself, and Qiao did NOT claim mathematical equality of barycenter and pooled mixture covariance. Rezaei et al ICLR2026 original https://proceedings.iclr.cc/paper_files/paper/2026/file/97c5b2707228e7e3fb67e4ecc2e0e607-Paper-Conference.pdf Thm4.1/4.3 source PDF p4–5 screenshots: target covariance shapes limiting downstream risk under strict model assumptions; no violation of either theorem alleged.
**Already-known repair:** weighted law of total covariance `Sigma_pool=sum lambda Sigma_k+sum lambda(mu_k−mu_bar)(mu_k−mu_bar)^T` can be computed through standard confidential aggregation/MPC; add correctly budgeted DP if result released with formal privacy protection. Strong direct DP prior art d'Orsi–Novikov ALT2026 `On Purely Private Covariance Estimation` https://proceedings.mlr.press/v313/d-orsi26a.html (optimal spectral/Frobenius private covariance), Gu et al arXiv2510.03021 `Differentially Private Wasserstein Barycenters` already DP OT source; 2026 Dalery et al constrained Gaussian mixture barycenters. No *new* end-to-end selection protocol or quantified privacy/communication/risk advantage proven.
